Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the models framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 249. The resulting operator achieves bound O(15/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to models structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 249/15 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 845 + O(1/n^870). Summing over all modes and applying the models structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
